CAECMATH Answer Keys and Explanations

Numbered to match the practice tests in your printed study guide.

Practice Test 1 Answer Key

  1. The correct answer is C (The probability of drawing a red tile is 4 out of 9.): Count the total number of tiles, so 5 blue plus 4 red equals 9 tiles. The favourable outcomes are the 4 red tiles. So the probability is 4 out of 9. The other choices use the wrong number as the top or bottom of the fraction.
  2. The correct answer is D (The mode of the data is 12 years old.): The mode is the value that appears most often in the data set. Count how many times each number appears in the list 12, 15, 12, 14, 12, 11. The number 12 appears three times, which is more than any other number. So the mode, or most common value, is 12.
  3. The correct answer is B (The total cost to send the parcel is 28 dollars.): Multiply the per kilogram fee by the weight, so 2 times 8 equals 16. Add the flat fee of 12 to that, so 12 plus 16 equals 28. The value 96 comes from multiplying 12 by 8, which forgets the flat fee addition. The value 16 is only the per kilogram charge and misses the flat fee.
  4. The correct answer is C (The circumference of the pool is 31.4 metres.): Circumference is pi times the diameter, so use 3.14 times 10. The product is 31.4, and the answer is in metres. The value 15.7 comes from using pi times the radius instead of the diameter. The value 62.8 comes from multiplying 2 times pi times the diameter, which doubles the distance.
  5. The correct answer is A (The diver is 16 metres below the surface.): Treat going down as a negative move and going up as a positive move. Starting at zero, go down 12 to negative 12, up 5 to negative 7, then down 9 to negative 16. Negative 16 means the diver is 16 metres below the surface. Choosing 8 comes from adding 12 and 5 before subtracting the 9, which is not the path taken.
  6. The correct answer is D (The total cost of the paint is 53 dollars.): Read the price of each colour from the table, so white is 12, blue is 14, and green is 15. Two cans of white cost 2 times 12, which is 24. Add 24 plus 14 plus 15 to get 53. The value 41 comes from buying only one can of white.
  7. The correct answer is C (The value of x in the equation 2 times x plus 5 equals 17 is 6.): Subtract 5 from both sides of the equation to get 2x equals 12. Then divide both sides by 2 to get x equals 6. So the value of x is 6. Choice B is the value of 2 times x, not the value of x alone.
  8. The correct answer is 1/4 cup: Three quarters is three fourths of a cup, and one half is two fourths of a cup. Subtract the amount already added from the amount the recipe needs, so three fourths minus two fourths equals one fourth. The answer one fourth is already in lowest terms.
  9. The correct answer is C (The value of the entire expression is 9.): Do the brackets first, so 3 plus 4 equals 7. Then multiply 2 by 7 to get 14, and finally subtract 5 to get 9. The value 9 is the result of following the brackets first. The value 4 comes from subtracting before multiplying, and 7 comes from stopping after the brackets. The value 11 comes from an error in the final subtraction.
  10. The correct answer is 43.96 metres: The circumference of a circle is twice pi times the radius. Twice the radius of 7 is 14, then multiply 14 by 3.14 to get 43.96. The correct circumference is 43.96 metres. Checking the math, 2 times 3.14 times 7 equals 43.96.
  11. The correct answer is 11 degrees Celsius: Find the warmest temperature of 19 and the coolest temperature of 8 in the list. Subtract the lowest from the highest, so 19 minus 8 equals 11. The range between the readings is 11 degrees Celsius. The range measures the spread of the numbers, not the middle value.
  12. The correct answer is A (The greatest of the four numbers is 0.55): Compare the decimals by looking at the digits after the decimal point. The value 0.55 has a 5 in the tenths place and a 5 in the hundredths place. The value 0.5 has only a 5 in the tenths place and nothing more. So 0.55 is larger than all of 0.5, 0.45, and 0.405.
  13. The correct answer is 24 boxes: Each box weighs 2.5 kilograms, so divide the total load by the weight of one box. Divide 60 by 2.5, which is the same as dividing 600 by 25. That division gives 24. So there are 24 boxes on the truck.
  14. The correct answer is C (The distance between the two points is 10 units): Point A is at negative 2 and point B is at positive 8. Distance is the larger value minus the smaller value, so 8 minus negative 2 equals 10. The 6 comes from ignoring the sign and treating point A as 2, and the 2 is the magnitude of point A, the absolute value of negative 2.
  15. The correct answer is A (The output beside the input 4 is 16.): Use the rule that the output is the input squared, since the table shows 2 becomes 4 and 3 becomes 9. So the output for the input 4 is 4 squared. Four squared equals 16, so the missing output is 16. The other choices come from doubling the input, or from a different rule.
  16. The correct answer is B (The gym is found to be 36 feet long.): One yard equals 3 feet, so multiply the number of yards by 3. Multiply 12 yards by 3 to get 36 feet. The correct length is 36 feet. The choice of 15 comes from adding the yards and the feet, and the choice of 9 comes from dividing instead of multiplying. The choice of 120 comes from using 10 feet per yard by mistake.
  17. The correct answer is 4: Do the multiplication before the subtraction. Multiply 2 by 5 to get 10, then subtract 10 from 14 to get 4. The correct value is 4. Checking the order, 14 minus 10 equals 4, so the expression equals 4.
  18. The correct answer is C (The area of the triangular garden is 30 square metres.): The area of a triangle is half the base times the height, so take half of 10 and multiply by 6. Half of 10 is 5, and 5 times 6 is 30, so the area is 30 square metres. The value 60 comes from using the full base without taking half. The value 16 comes from adding the base and height, and 17 uses the perimeter.
  19. The correct answer is C (The median of the given data set is 8.): Put the numbers in order from smallest to largest, which gives 3, 6, 8, 8, and 10. The middle value is the third number, which is 8. So the median is 8. Choosing 6 comes from taking the second value after ordering, the one just below the median.
  20. The correct answer is 24 $: Convert the percent discount to a decimal, so 25 percent becomes 0.25. Multiply the marked price by that decimal, so 96 times 0.25 equals 24. The customer saves 24 dollars, and would pay 72 dollars after the discount. A check shows that 25 percent is one quarter, and one quarter of 96 is 24.
  21. The correct answer is A (Each of the crates holds 4 bottles of juice.): The total of 23 bottles includes the 3 extra on the shelf, so subtract 3 from 23 to get 20. Divide 20 by the 5 crates to find 4 bottles in each crate. The correct answer is 4 bottles per crate. The choice of 5 comes from dividing by the wrong number, and the choice of 3 comes from subtracting the extra bottles before dividing incorrectly.
  22. The correct answer is B (The final price the customer pays is 67 dollars and 80 cents.): Find the discount first, 25 percent of 80 equals 20, so the sale price is 80 minus 20 equals 60. Add tax on the sale price, 13 percent of 60 equals 7.80. Add the tax to the sale price, 60 plus 7.80 equals 67.80. Choosing 70 dollars and 40 cents comes from computing tax on the original 80 dollars.
  23. The correct answer is B (The probability of drawing a blue marble is one half): There are 5 blue marbles out of a total of 3 plus 5 plus 2, which is 10 marbles. The probability is the number of blue divided by the total, so 5 divided by 10. Five over 10 simplifies to one half.
  24. The correct answer is B (The mean of the four quiz scores is 84): The mean is found by adding all the scores and then dividing by how many there are. Add 78 plus 82 plus 86 plus 90 to get 336. Divide 336 by 4 scores to get 84.
  25. The correct answer is D (The new height of the photo is 24 centimetres): A scale factor of 3 means every length is multiplied by 3. Multiply the original height of 8 by 3 to get 24 centimetres. The value 15 comes from multiplying the width by 3 instead. The value 11 comes from adding the scale factor to the height.
  26. The correct answer is A (The mean of the five quiz marks is 74): Add the five marks together, so 72 plus 68 plus 80 plus 76 plus 74 equals 370. Divide the total by the number of quizzes, which is 5. Three hundred seventy divided by 5 is 74, so the mean is 74.
  27. The correct answer is A (The probability of two threes is 1 over 16): The two spins are independent, so the probabilities are multiplied. The chance of landing on 3 on one spin is 1 over 4. Multiply 1 over 4 by 1 over 4 to get 1 over 16. The value 1 over 4 would only be the chance of a single spin.
  28. The correct answer is A (The owner has 31.25 dollars left in the wallet.): Subtract the grocery cost from the starting amount. Subtract 18.75 from 50, so take 18 from 50 to get 32, and then subtract the extra 0.75 to get 31.25. The correct amount left is 31.25 dollars. The choice of 32.25 comes from subtracting the cents incorrectly, and the choice of 31.75 comes from borrowing the wrong way. The choice of 30.25 comes from a borrowing error in the cents when subtracting 18.75 from 50.
  29. The correct answer is 38: The range is the largest value minus the smallest value. The largest value is 42 and the smallest is 4. Subtract 4 from 42 to get 38. Adding the values together gives 108, which is the sum and is not the range.
  30. The correct answer is D (The perimeter of the plot is 60 metres): The perimeter is the sum of all three sides of the triangle. Add 15, 20, and 25 to get 60 metres. The value 45 adds only two of the sides. The value 50 could come from a mis-added total.
  31. The correct answer is A (The new price of the tablet is 300 dollars.): A 25 percent price drop means the customer pays 75 percent of the original price. Take 25 percent of 400, which is 100, and subtract it from 400 to get 300. The correct new price is 300 dollars. The choice of 100 is the discount amount, and the choice of 375 comes from subtracting only 25 dollars. The choice of 325 comes from taking off only 75 dollars by mistake.
  32. The correct answer is B (Maya receives 3.85 dollars in change from the cashier.): Add the two item costs to find the total, so 12.75 plus 8.40 equals 21.15 dollars. Subtract the total from the 25 dollar bill, so 25 minus 21.15 equals 3.85. Maya receives 3.85 dollars in change. The choice of 3.95 comes from a small addition error that makes the total 21.05 instead of 21.15.
  33. The correct answer is C (The probability of choosing an orange is 2 out of 5.): Count the total number of fruit, so 2 oranges plus 3 apples equals 5 pieces. The favourable outcomes are the 2 oranges. So the probability is 2 out of 5. The other choices swap the numbers, or use the wrong total.
  34. The correct answer is B (The ninth figure has 36 dots.): Put the figure number 9 into the rule, so the dots equal 4 times 9. Multiply 4 by 9 to get 36. The value 40 comes from multiplying 4 by 10 instead. The value 45 comes from multiplying 5 by 9.
  35. The correct answer is 80 cells: The pattern multiplies each term by 2 to get the next one. Take the last count, 40, and multiply it by 2. This gives 80, so the count after the next doubling is 80. A common error is to add 5 each time, which would give 45.
  36. The correct answer is 33 dollars: Subtract the fee from the starting balance. Subtract 12 from 45 to get 33. The correct new balance is 33 dollars. Checking, 45 minus 12 is 33, so the account holds 33 dollars after the fee.
  37. The correct answer is B (The area of the triangle is 30 square centimetres.): The area of a triangle is one half of the base times the height. Multiply the base of 12 by the height of 5 to get 60, then take half to get 30. The value 60 comes from forgetting the one half in the formula. Adding the base and height gives 17, which is not the area.
  38. The correct answer is D (The real distance between the towns is 30 kilometres.): A ratio of 1 to 5 means every centimetre on the map stands for 5 kilometres on the ground. Multiply the map distance of 6 by 5 to get 30. The value 11 comes from adding the scale number to the distance. The value 6 comes from using the map distance only.
  39. The correct answer is B (The total simple interest still owed is $90): Simple interest is the principal times the rate times the time in years. Multiply 600 by 0.05 to get 30 dollars of interest for one year. Multiply that 30 by the 3 years to get 90 dollars. The value 30 is only the interest for a single year.
  40. The correct answer is D (The baker needs 6 cups of sugar altogether.): Multiply the amount for one batch by the number of batches, so 3/4 times 8. Write 3/4 times 8 as 3 times 8 divided by 4, which is 24 divided by 4. The quotient 24 divided by 4 is 6, so the baker needs 6 cups of sugar. The other choices come from adding the fractions, using the wrong multiplier, or an error scaling the recipe.
  41. The correct answer is A (The range of the daily sales is 260 dollars.): The range is the largest value minus the smallest value, which is 300 minus 40. That gives 260 dollars. The single high sale on the last day stretches the range a great deal. The value 40 comes from taking just the lowest sale, and 300 comes from taking just the highest.
  42. The correct answer is C (The length of the hypotenuse is 15 centimetres.): Use the Pythagorean theorem, where the square of the hypotenuse equals the sum of the squares of the two legs. Square 9 to get 81 and square 12 to get 144, then add them to get 225. The square root of 225 is 15, so the hypotenuse is 15 centimetres. The value 21 comes from adding the two legs, and 108 comes from multiplying them.

Practice Test 2 Answer Key

  1. The correct answer is C (The printer prints 105 pages in 7 minutes.): Find the unit rate by dividing the pages by the minutes, so 45 divided by 3 is 15 pages per minute. Multiply the rate of 15 pages per minute by 7 minutes to get the total. Since 15 times 7 is 105, the printer prints 105 pages in 7 minutes. The choice of 90 comes from using 4 minutes instead.
  2. The correct answer is B (The value of the whole expression is 9.): Do the multiplication and division before adding and subtracting. Multiplying 4 times 2 gives 8, and dividing 6 by 3 gives 2. The expression then reads 3 plus 8 minus 2, which equals 9. Choosing 12 comes from adding 3 and 4 first, which breaks the order of operations.
  3. The correct answer is C (The new mean is 12.5, since 50 divided by 4 is 12.5): If the mean of three numbers is 10, their sum is 10 times 3, which equals 30. Add the new number 20 to get a total of 50. Divide 50 by 4 numbers to get 12.5.
  4. The correct answer is C (The number of students who scored 20 to 29 is 12.): A histogram groups data into ranges, and the bar for a range shows how many values fall into it. Find the range 20 to 29 and read the number attached to it, which is 12. So 12 students scored in the 20 to 29 range. The choice of 20 is the total of all the students, and 8 is the count for the 10 to 19 range.
  5. The correct answer is 12 °C: Put the five temperatures in order, which they already are, and find the middle value. The ordered list has two values below and two above the centre, and the centre is 12. The median is the third of the five values, so it is 12 degrees Celsius. The values 9 and 14 are the second and fourth numbers, not the middle.
  6. The correct answer is B (The perimeter of the garden plot is 24 metres.): The perimeter of a rectangle is twice the length plus twice the width. Double the length of 7 to get 14, and double the width of 5 to get 10, then add them to get 24. The correct perimeter is 24 metres. The choice of 12 comes from adding only one length and one width, and the choice of 35 is the area. The choice of 17 comes from counting one side twice but missing one side.
  7. The correct answer is D (The median age of these six is 13 years): With six ages, the median is the average of the third and fourth values. The third value is 12 and the fourth is 14. The average of 12 and 14 is 13. That is the median age of the six people.
  8. The correct answer is 14 tiles: The pattern rises by 3 tiles each row, so the counts are 2, 5, 8, 11, and 14. Count down the rows: row one is 2, row two is 5, row three is 8, row four is 11, and row five is 14. The fifth row has 14 tiles, which is 3 more than the fourth row. The answer is 14 tiles.
  9. The correct answer is B (The circumference of the garden is 31.4 metres.): Multiply pi by the diameter to find the circumference. Using 3.14 for pi and a diameter of 10, compute 3.14 times 10. This gives 31.4, so the circumference is 31.4 metres. Choosing 78.5 comes from finding the area with a radius of 5 instead.
  10. The correct answer is 7 km: Write the cost rule as 3 plus 2 times the kilometres equals 17. Subtract the flat fee of 3 from 17 to get the kilometre charge, which is 14. Divide 14 by the 2 dollars per kilometre to get 7 kilometres.
  11. The correct answer is D (Ben read a total of 4 books for the month.): Find the bar labelled Ben on the horizontal axis. Read the height of that bar against the vertical axis to see the value 4. The value 2 is Ana's count and 6 is Cate's count. Reading across the wrong bar is the most common error on this type of question.
  12. The correct answer is 120 juice boxes: Multiply the juice boxes per student by the number of students. Take 4 boxes times 30 students, which gives 120. The school orders 120 juice boxes in total. Four times 3 is 12, and adding a zero gives 120.
  13. The correct answer is B (The real room is found to be 12 metres long.): The scale says each centimetre on the drawing represents 2 metres in real life. Multiply the drawing length of 6 centimetres by 2 metres to get 12. The correct real length is 12 metres. The choice of 8 comes from adding the centimetres and the metres, and the choice of 3 comes from dividing by 2. The choice of 18 comes from multiplying by the wrong number.
  14. The correct answer is D (The side length of the square is 11 cm.): A square has four equal sides, so divide the perimeter by 4. The perimeter is 44 cm, so 44 divided by 4. The result is 11, so each side of the square is 11 cm. The other choices come from multiplying the perimeter, or from a division error.
  15. The correct answer is C (The order from least to greatest is 1/2, 3/5, 2/3.): Change each fraction to a decimal to compare them easily. The fraction 1/2 is 0.5, 3/5 is 0.6, and 2/3 is about 0.67. From smallest to largest the order is 0.5, 0.6, and 0.67, which matches 1/2, 3/5, and 2/3. The other lists place one of the fractions out of order.
  16. The correct answer is 13: The rule subtracts 4 from each term to get the next one. Take the last term, 17, and subtract 4 from it. This gives 13, so the next term is 13. A common error is to add 4 instead, which would give 21.
  17. The correct answer is 314 cm²: The area of a circle is pi times the radius squared, so first square the radius to get 10 times 10, which is 100. Multiply 3.14 by 100 to get 314. The area of the circular table is 314 square centimetres. The radius is 10, so its square is 100, and pi times 100 is 314.
  18. The correct answer is A (The area of the square floor is 49 square metres.): A square has four equal sides, so area is side times side. Multiply 7 by 7 to get 49 square metres. Adding the four sides, 7 plus 7 plus 7 plus 7, gives 28, which is the perimeter. The value 14 comes from doubling one side, and 56 comes from multiplying 7 by 8 by mistake.
  19. The correct answer is A (The total after 7 weeks is 15 kilometres.): Substitute 7 in place of the weeks in the rule, so 2 times 7 is 14. Then add the 1 from the rule to get 15. The total is 15 kilometres. The value 14 comes from leaving off the plus 1 step.
  20. The correct answer is 88: Put the five scores in order from smallest to largest, which gives 76, 84, 88, 92, and 95. The list has an odd number of values, so there is one middle score. The middle value is 88, so the median score is 88. A common error is to average all the scores, which gives the mean rather than the median.
  21. The correct answer is 24 cubic m: The volume of a rectangular prism is found by multiplying length, width, and height. Multiply 4 times 3 to get 12, then multiply 12 by 2 to get 24. The answer is kept as cubic metres because all three measures are in metres.
  22. The correct answer is 120 cm3: Multiply the three dimensions together to find the volume. Multiplying 8 times 5 gives 40, and then 40 times 3 gives 120. The volume is 120 cubic centimetres. A common error is to add the three dimensions, which gives 16 instead.
  23. The correct answer is A (The final position of the diver is negative 3 metres.): Descending 7 metres is negative 7, and rising 4 metres means adding positive 4. Adding negative 7 and 4 is the same as subtracting 4 from 7, which gives negative 3. The correct final position is negative 3 metres. The choice of negative 11 comes from adding the two amounts, and the choice of 3 comes from losing the negative sign. The choice of 11 comes from adding the numbers as if both were positive.
  24. The correct answer is 1.20 $: Change the tax rate to a decimal, so 5 percent becomes 0.05. Multiply the price by that rate, 24 times 0.05 equals 1.20. The tax added to the book is 1.20 dollars. A common error is to move the decimal the wrong way and get 0.12 dollars.
  25. The correct answer is C (The volume is 24 cubic cm, since 4 times 3 times 2 is 24): The volume of a rectangular prism is length times width times height. Multiply 4 by 3 to get 12, then multiply by the height 2. Twelve times 2 equals 24 cubic centimetres, and 12 is only the base area.
  26. The correct answer is C (The value of the expression is 16.): First work out the power, so 2 squared is 4. Then multiply by 3, so 4 times 3 is 12. Finally add 4 to get 16. The value 12 comes from stopping after the multiplication.
  27. The correct answer is D (The courier earns 81 dollars for 9 parcels.): Find the earnings per parcel by dividing 54 dollars by 6 parcels, which gives 9 dollars per parcel. Multiply the rate of 9 dollars per parcel by 9 parcels to get 81 dollars. The courier earns 81 dollars. The choice of 90 comes from using 10 dollars per parcel instead of 9.
  28. The correct answer is B (The percent who chose basketball is 37.5 percent.): Divide the students who chose basketball by the total, so 15 divided by 40 equals 0.375. Multiply by 100 to change the decimal to a percent, giving 37.5 percent. The value 35 is close but comes from a slightly different fraction. The value 40 is the total number of students, not the percent.
  29. The correct answer is A (The length of the hypotenuse is 15 cm.): Use the Pythagorean theorem, where the hypotenuse squared equals the sum of the leg squares. The legs are 9 cm and 12 cm, so the hypotenuse squared is 81 plus 144. Eighty one plus 144 is 225, and the square root of 225 is 15 cm. The other choices come from adding the legs, or from squaring only one leg.
  30. The correct answer is A (The perimeter of the rectangle is 24 centimetres.): Perimeter is the distance around the shape, so add the length and width then double it. Seven plus five is 12, and doubling that gives 24. The value 35 is the area, found by multiplying 7 by 5. The value 12 is only the length plus the width before doubling.
  31. The correct answer is D (The mean of the five test scores is 86.4 in the class.): Add all five scores to get a total of 432. Divide 432 by the five scores to get 86.4, so the mean is 86.4. The one 100 pulls the mean above the middle value because it is a high outlier. Choice B is the median, which is the middle score when the list is ordered.
  32. The correct answer is D (The jogger runs 300 metres around the field.): The perimeter of a rectangle is twice the length plus twice the width. Twice the length of 100 is 200, and twice the width of 50 is 100, so adding them gives 300. The correct distance is 300 metres. The choice of 5000 is the area, and the choice of 150 comes from adding only one length and one width. The choice of 250 comes from leaving out one of the widths.
  33. The correct answer is 37.5 %: There are 8 equal sections in total, and 3 are blue, so the probability is 3 out of 8. Divide 3 by 8 to get 0.375, then convert the decimal to a percent by multiplying by 100. So the probability is 37.5 percent. That means landing on blue happens 37.5 out of every 100 spins.
  34. The correct answer is A (The value of x in the equation is 7.): Subtract 6 from both sides to get 2x equals 14. Divide both sides by 2 to isolate x, so x equals 14 over 2. Dividing 14 by 2 gives 7. Choosing 8 comes from subtracting 4 instead of 6 before dividing.
  35. The correct answer is A (The relationship shows more hours of study leading to higher quiz scores.): The description says that as the hours of study go up, the quiz scores also go up, and there is no sharp turn. This is a positive relationship, where more study lines up with higher scores. The larger pattern matches the option that says more hours lead to higher scores. A negative relationship would show scores dropping as hours rise.
  36. The correct answer is B (The unit price of one apple is 1.25 dollars.): The unit price is the total cost divided by the number of items. Divide 5 dollars by 4 apples, which gives 1.25 dollars per apple. The correct unit price is 1.25 dollars. The choice of 20 comes from multiplying the price by the number of apples, and the choice of 0.20 comes from inverting the ratio. The choice of 9 comes from adding the price and the number of apples.
  37. The correct answer is C (The measurement being described here is the volume): The volume is the amount of space a three dimensional object fills. It uses the length, width, and height of the object together. Area is only a two dimensional surface measurement. Perimeter is a one dimensional distance around a shape.
  38. The correct answer is B (The line has 6 people, since 3 plus 4 minus 1 is 6): Count the people in front of Ali plus the people behind him plus Ali himself. Adding 3 and 4 counts Ali twice because he is both third from the front and fourth from the back. Subtract the one extra count, giving 3 plus 4 minus 1 equals 6 people.
  39. The correct answer is 204 dollars: Multiply the hourly rate by the hours worked, so 12 times 17. One way is 12 times 10 is 120 and 12 times 7 is 84, and 120 plus 84 equals 204. The student earns 204 dollars that week. A common mistake is to add the two numbers instead of multiplying.
  40. The correct answer is A (The probability of drawing a number less than 4 is 3/10.): The numbers less than 4 are 1, 2, and 3, so there are three favourable balls. There are 10 balls in total, so the probability is 3 out of 10. Simplify is not needed because 3 and 10 have no common factor. The value 4/10 would include the ball numbered 4, which is not less than 4.
  41. The correct answer is C (The area of the garden is 36 square metres.): Multiply the length by the width, so 12 times 3 equals 36. Since both units are metres, the area is in square metres. Adding 12 and 3 gives 15, which is the length plus the width. The value 30 comes from using 2 times 15, which is part of the perimeter calculation.
  42. The correct answer is B (The area of the circular table is 28.26 square metres.): The area of a circle is pi times the radius squared, so square the radius to get 3 times 3, which is 9. Multiply 3.14 by 9 to get 28.26 square metres. The value 18.84 is the circumference, which uses the diameter instead of the radius squared. The value 9.42 uses the radius once, and 12.56 comes from a different radius.

Practice Test 3 Answer Key

  1. The correct answer is D (The volume of the box is 36 cubic centimetres.): Multiply the length, width, and height together, so 6 times 3 times 2. First find 6 times 3, which is 18, and then multiply 18 by 2 to get 36. The volume is 36 cubic centimetres. The value 18 comes from multiplying only the length and width, the value 11 is the sum of the three dimensions (6+3+2), and the value 22 is twice that sum.
  2. The correct answer is B (The average speed of the bus is 60 kilometres per hour.): Divide the total distance by the total time, so 240 divided by 4. The bus travels 240 kilometres in 4 hours, and 240 over 4 is 60. The value 40 comes from dividing by 6 hours by mistake. The value 80 comes from multiplying the distance and time in the wrong order.
  3. The correct answer is 40 customers: Add the five counts together to get 20 plus 30 plus 40 plus 50 plus 60 equals 200. Divide that total by the five stores, so 200 divided by 5 equals 40. The mean number of customers is 40. The numbers rise by 10 each time, so the average sits in the middle at 40.
  4. The correct answer is B (The volume of the container is 125 cubic metres): The volume of a cube is the side length multiplied by itself three times. Multiply 5 times 5 to get 25, then multiply 25 by 5 to get 125. The volume is 125 cubic metres. The value 25 is the area of one face, not the volume.
  5. The correct answer is B (The median age of the patients is 50 years.): The five ages are already in order from smallest to largest. With an odd count, the median is the middle value, which is the third one, 50. The value 45 is the second and 55 is the fourth, so neither is the middle. The value 60 is the oldest patient, not the median.
  6. The correct answer is 5 cakes: The mode is the number that appears most often. The number 5 occurs three times, which is more than any other value. A quick check shows 5 is the most frequent sale. The mode is 5 cakes.
  7. The correct answer is 48 centimetres: A square has four equal sides, so multiply one side by 4. Twelve times 4 is 48. A quick check confirms that 12 plus 12 plus 12 plus 12 is 48. The frame is 48 centimetres around.
  8. The correct answer is D (There is 0.5 litres of fluid left in the container.): Subtract the amount removed from the amount that was there. Subtract 0.25 from 0.75, which is the same as 75 hundredths minus 25 hundredths, giving 50 hundredths. The correct amount left is 0.5 litres. The choice of 0.25 is the amount removed, and the choice of 1.0 comes from adding the two amounts. The choice of 0.05 comes from a misplaced decimal.
  9. The correct answer is A (The surface area of the cube is 96 square units.): Find the area of one face by squaring the edge length, so 4 times 4 equals 16. Multiply that face area by the number of faces, 6 times 16 equals 96. The surface area is 96 square units. Choosing 64 comes from finding the volume, which is 4 cubed.
  10. The correct answer is C (The interest earned on the deposit is 80 dollars.): Write 4 percent as the decimal 0.04 and multiply it by the deposit, so 2000 times 0.04. Since 0.04 times 2000 is 80, the interest earned is 80 dollars. The value 40 comes from using 2 percent, and 60 comes from using 3 percent. The value 120 comes from using 6 percent.
  11. The correct answer is D (The volume of the box is 120 cubic cm.): The volume of a rectangular box is length times width times height. Multiply 6 times 5 to get 30, then multiply by 4. Thirty times 4 equals 120, so the box holds 120 cubic cm. The other choices come from using two dimensions only, or from a multiplication error.
  12. The correct answer is C (The cook needs 8 parts of nuts for 12 parts of raisins.): The ratio of nuts to raisins is 2 to 3, so set 2/3 equal to the unknown nuts over 12. Practice Test 4 Answer Key

  1. The correct answer is A (The median number of minutes is 18.): Put the seven numbers in order from smallest to largest, and the middle value is the one with three numbers on each side.   2. The correct answer is 9 degrees: Find the largest temperature, which is 12, and the smallest temperature, which is 3. Subtract the smallest from the largest, 12 minus 3 equals 9. The range is 9 degrees. A common error is to use the mean, which is 7, instead of the range.
  3. The correct answer is B (The probability of not landing on blue is 5/8.): There are 8 equal sections in total, and 3 of them are blue. The choice of 3/8 is the probability of landing on blue, and 2/8 is only the red sections.
  4. The correct answer is B (The value of the next term in the pattern is 19.): The rule adds 4 to each term to get the next one. Take the last known term, 15, and add 4 to it. This gives 19, so the next term is 19. Choosing 18 comes from adding only 3 instead of 4.
  5. The correct answer is A (The area of the hot tub is 12.56 square metres.): The area of a circle uses the radius, so first take half of the diameter of 4 to get a radius of 2. The choice of 25.12 comes from using the diameter as the radius, and the choice of 6.28 comes from multiplying pi times the radius only. The choice of 50.24 comes from squaring the diameter by mistake.
  6. The correct answer is D (The salesperson earns 72 dollars in commission.): Write 8 percent as 0.08 and multiply by the sales total, so 0.08 times 900 is 72. The commission is 72 dollars.   12. The correct answer is C (The probability of flipping heads and rolling a 6 is 1 out of 12.): The two events are independent, so multiply their probabilities.   13. The correct answer is D (The result of the expression is 7.): BEDMAS says to work out the power first, so 2 cubed is 2 times 2 times 2, which is 8.   32. The correct answer is D (The wall is found to be 12 metres high.): The ladder is the hypotenuse, and the ground distance is one leg, so find the other leg using the Pythagorean theorem. Square 13 to get 169 and square 5 to get 25, then subtract 25 from 169 to get 144. The square root of 144 is 12, so the wall is 12 metres high. The choice of 18 comes from adding the two known sides (13 + 5), and the choice of 8 comes from subtracting them. The choice of 16 comes from a miscount.
  33. The correct answer is A (The area of the rectangular poster is 5400 square centimetres.): The area of a rectangle is length times width, so multiply 90 by 60. Since 90 times 60 is 5400, the poster covers 5400 square centimetres. The answer is in square units because it is an area. Choice C adds the two sides, which gives a perimeter, not an area.
  34. The correct answer is A (The value of 3 squared plus 4 squared is 25.): Square each number before adding, so 3 squared is 9 and 4 squared is 16. Add the two squares, 9 plus 16, to get 25. The value 49 comes from squaring the sum of 7 first instead. The value 14 comes from doubling the two numbers without squaring them.
  35. The correct answer is B (The temperature at midnight is negative 6 degrees): The temperature starts at 8 degrees above zero and then drops by 14 degrees. Moving 14 degrees down from 8 lands at negative 6 degrees. The value 6 would come from treating the drop as 8 minus 14 but ignoring the negative sign. The value negative 22 would come from adding the two amounts instead of subtracting.
  36. The correct answer is A (The volume of the box is 72000 cubic centimetres.): Multiply the length, width, and height together, so 60 times 40 is 2400. Then multiply that by the height of 30, so 2400 times 30 is 72000. The volume is 72000 cubic centimetres. The value 7200 comes from multiplying 60, 40, and 3 and leaving off one zero.
  37. The correct answer is B (The total cost of the items is 16 dollars.): Multiply the price of each item by the number bought, then add the results. Bread is 3 times 2, which is 6 dollars, milk is 4 times 1, which is 4 dollars, and apples are 2 times 3, which is 6 dollars. Adding 6, 4, and 6 gives 16 dollars in total. The choice of 12 comes from forgetting to multiply by the quantities for some items.
  38. The correct answer is A (The volume of the shed is 60 cubic metres.): The volume of a rectangular shed is length times width times height. Multiply 5 by 4 to get 20, then multiply 20 by 3 to get 60. The correct volume is 60 cubic metres. The choice of 12 comes from adding the three dimensions, and the choice of 20 comes from multiplying only the length and width. The choice of 94 is the surface area of the shed, which is a different measure.
  39. The correct answer is A (The probability of drawing a green marble is 1/4.): Probability is the number of favourable outcomes divided by the total number of outcomes. There are 5 green marbles and 20 marbles in total, so the probability is 5 out of 20. Divide the top and bottom by 5 to simplify to 1/4. The choice of 1/5 comes from using the pieces of the fraction the wrong way around.
  40. The correct answer is 6 $: Add the four donations together, 4 plus 6 plus 9 plus 5 equals 24. Divide the total by the number of donations, 24 divided by 4 equals 6. The mean donation is 6 dollars. A common error is to divide by the wrong number of donations.
  41. The correct answer is A (The perimeter of the room is 24 metres in total.): Walk around the outer edge of the L shape and add each side. The sides are 6, 4, 3, 2, 3, and 6 metres long, going around the outside. Adding them, 6 plus 4 plus 3 plus 2 plus 3 plus 6 equals 24. Choosing 30 comes from adding the two full perimeters of the separate rectangles.
  42. The correct answer is B (The amount of sales tax is 6.60 dollars.): Write 12 percent as 0.12 and multiply by the food price, so 0.12 times 55 is 6.60. The tax is 6.60 dollars. The value 12.00 comes from treating 12 percent as 12 dollars. The value 6.05 comes from a decimal error.

Practice Test 8 Answer Key

  1. The correct answer is 15 cm: The two legs are 9 and 12, so square them and add. Nine squared is 81 and 12 squared is 144, which add to 225. The square root of 225 is 15. So the hypotenuse is 15 centimetres.
  2. The correct answer is D (The team ran 12.5 kilometres across the three days.): Convert the mixed number distance to a decimal before adding, so 3.5 plus 4.25 plus 4.75. Add the decimals in steps, so 3.5 plus 4.25 is 7.75. Add 4.75 to 7.75 to get 12.5, so the team runs 12.5 kilometres in total. The wrong choices come from rounding one of the distances up, or from adding the whole numbers only.
  3. The correct answer is A (The marker labelled C sits at negative 1.): Locate negative 1 on the number line between negative 2 and 0. The marker sitting at that spot carries the label C. So marker C is at negative 1. The other markers sit at negative 3, positive 2, and positive 4 instead.
  4. The correct answer is C (The volume of the tank is 30000 cubic centimetres.): Multiply the length, width, and height, so 50 times 30 is 1500, and 1500 times 20 is 30000. The value 3000 comes from dropping a zero, and 60000 comes from multiplying the first two numbers twice. The value 100 comes from adding the three measurements.
  5. The correct answer is D (The ladder is 10 metres long from the ground to the wall.): For a right triangle, the square of the longest side equals the sum of the squares of the two shorter sides. Square 6 to get 36 and square 8 to get 64, then add to get 100. The square root of 100 is 10, so the ladder is 10 metres long. Choice D stops at 100 and forgets to take the square root.
  6. The correct answer is C (The area of the window is 3 square metres.): The area of a rectangle is length times width, so multiply the width of 1.5 metres by the height of 2 metres. One and a half times 2 is 3, so the correct area is 3 square metres. The choice of 3.5 comes from adding the width and height, and the choice of 1.5 is just the width. The choice of 4 comes from multiplying 2 by 2 by mistake.
  7. The correct answer is B (The tank contains 150 litres of water when filled): Three quarters means three fourths, so multiply the full capacity by 3 and divide by 4. Two hundred times 3 equals 600, and 600 divided by 4 equals 150. The 50 is one quarter, the 75 is three quarters of 100, and 100 is one half of the tank.
  8. The correct answer is C (The regular price of the calculator is 50 dollars.): A 10 percent discount means the sale price is 90 percent of the regular price. Divide the sale price of 45 by 0.90, so 45 divided by 0.90 equals 50. A common error is to add 10 percent of 45, which gives 49.50 instead. Checking the work, 10 percent of 50 is 5, and 50 minus 5 is 45.
  9. The correct answer is C (The customer sent 200 text messages in that billing month.): Subtract the 15 dollar activation fee from the 25 dollar bill to get 10 dollars.   10. The correct answer is C (A cube has a total of 6 faces.): A cube is a three dimensional shape with six flat square faces. Count the top, the bottom, and the four sides around it to get six faces. The correct answer is 6 faces. The choice of 4 comes from counting only the side faces, and the choice of 8 comes from counting the corners. The choice of 12 comes from counting the edges instead of the faces.
  11. The correct answer is 840 dollars: Multiply the hourly rate by the number of hours worked. The rate of 24 times 35 hours is 840. Break the multiplication into 24 times 30 which is 720, plus 24 times 5 which is 120. Adding 720 and 120 gives 840 dollars for the week.
  12. The correct answer is 11 degrees: The range is the highest value minus the lowest value in a set. The highest temperature is 20 degrees and the lowest is 9 degrees. Subtract 9 from 20 to get 11, so the range is 11 degrees. The range measures how spread out the daily highs are.
  13. The correct answer is A (The real distance between the cities is 150 kilometres): The scale says 1 centimetre equals 25 kilometres. Multiply the map distance of 6 by 25. Six times 25 is 150 kilometres. The value 125 would come from using 5 centimetres instead of 6.
  14. The correct answer is D (According to the circle graph, 40 students ride the bus to school.): The circle graph is divided into four sections, and each section is labeled with a percent. The bus section takes up 40 percent of the whole circle. Because there are 100 students in total, 40 percent of 100 is 40 students. Choice A is the number who walk, and choice B is the number who bike.
  15. The correct answer is C (The area of the whole figure is 40 square units.): Find the area of the large square, 6 times 6 equals 36. Find the area of the small square, 2 times 2 equals 4. Add the two areas, 36 plus 4 equals 40. Choosing 32 comes from subtracting the small square's area instead of adding it.
  16. The correct answer is D (The probability of landing under 3 is 2/5.): There are 5 equal sections, and the numbers less than 3 are 1 and 2, which make 2 sections. The probability is 2 out of 5. The value 1/5 is the chance of landing on one single number. The value 3/5 includes the 3 as well, and 1/2 treats the spinner as if it had four sections.
  17. The correct answer is 15 %: Find the change by subtracting the old price from the new price, so 92 minus 80 equals 12. Divide the change by the original price, 12 divided by 80 equals 0.15. Move the decimal point two places to change 0.15 into 15 percent. The price increased by 15 percent.
  18. The correct answer is A (The sale price of the jacket is 30 dollars before any tax is added.): First find 25 percent of 40 by multiplying 40 by 0.25, which equals 10.   19. The correct answer is B (The probability of rolling greater than 4 is 1/3.): List the faces that are greater than 4, which are 5 and 6, so there are 2 successful faces.   20. The correct answer is C (The baker needs 3 full scoops of flour.): Dividing 3/4 by 1/4 means asking how many quarters are in three quarters. The choice of 2 comes from a miscount of the fraction, and the choice of 4 comes from thinking about a whole cup.   21. The correct answer is B (The real length of the room is 18 metres): The scale says 1 centimetre equals 2 metres. Multiply the drawing length of 9 by 2 to get the real length. Nine times 2 is 18 metres. The value 4.5 comes from dividing by the scale factor instead of multiplying.
  22. The correct answer is C (The probability of both events is 1 twelfth): The chance of heads is 1 out of 2, and the chance of rolling a 6 is 1 out of 6. For two independent events, multiply the two probabilities together. One half times one sixth equals 1 twelfth.

  24. The correct answer is 300 cm: Use the conversion rate that 1 metre equals 100 centimetres. Multiply the length in metres by 100, so 3 times 100 equals 300. The rope is 300 centimetres long. A common error is to divide 3 by 100, which gives 0.03.

  31. The correct answer is B (The probability of drawing red twice is 4/25.): The draws are independent because the marble is replaced, so multiply the two probabilities.   32. The correct answer is A (The volume of the prism is 300 cubic units.): Multiply the length, width, and height together. Multiplying 10 times 6 gives 60, and then 60 times 5 gives 300. The volume is 300 cubic units. Choosing 280 comes from finding the surface area rather than the volume.

  36. The correct answer is B (At least 96 cookies must be bought to serve the guests.): Each box holds 24 cookies.   37. The correct answer is A (The probability of rolling above a 4 is 1 third): The numbers greater than 4 on a die are 5 and 6, so there are 2 favourable outcomes. There are 6 possible outcomes in total. The probability is 2 out of 6, which simplifies to 1 third.

  42. The correct answer is 1/2: A standard die has 6 faces numbered 1 to 6. The numbers less than 4 are 1, 2, and 3, which is 3 favourable outcomes. The probability is 3 out of 6, which simplifies to 1 out of 2. So the probability of rolling less than 4 is 1/2.

Practice Test 9 Answer Key

  1. The correct answer is C (Three quarters of 80 is equal to 60.): Divide 80 by 4 to find one quarter, so 80 divided by 4 is 20. Multiply that by 3 to get three quarters, so 20 times 3 is 60. Three quarters of 80 is 60. The value 40 is one half of 80, and 45 is three quarters of 60. The value 64 comes from using 4/5 of 80 by mistake.
  2. The correct answer is A (The probability of both events is 1/6.): The chance of getting heads is 1/2, and the chance of rolling a number greater than 4 is 2 out of 6, which is 1/3. Because the two events are independent, multiply the probabilities, so 1/2 times 1/3 equals 1/6. The value 1/2 is just the coin result, and 1/3 is just the die result. The value 1/4 comes from treating the die as if it had four outcomes.
  3. The correct answer is 270 dollars: Multiply the monthly cost by the number of months in a year, so multiply 22.5 by 12. Twenty two point five times 12 equals 270. So the yearly total is 270 dollars.
  4. The correct answer is A (The probability of landing on blue is 3 out of 8.): There are 3 favourable sections that are blue and 8 sections in total. Probability is the favourable count divided by the total count, so 3 out of 8. The value 5 out of 8 comes from counting the non blue sections. The value 3 out of 5 comes from using the wrong total.
  5. The correct answer is C (The probability of rolling greater than 4 is 1/3.): There are two numbers greater than 4 on the die, which are 5 and 6, and six numbers in total. The probability is 2 out of 6, which reduces to 1/3 when both parts are divided by 2. The value 1/2 comes from counting three values that are at least 4. The value 1/6 is the chance of rolling one single number, and 2/3 doubles the result.
  6. The correct answer is D (The term after 24 in the sequence is 29.): Each term is found by adding 5 to the term before it. Take the last term, 24, and add 5 to it. Adding gives 29, so the next term is 29. Choosing 30 comes from adding 6 instead of 5.
  7. The correct answer is A (The shelf is 42 inches long, since 3.5 times 12 is 42): There are 12 inches in each foot, so multiply the number of feet by 12. Three point five times 12 equals 42. The shelf is 42 inches long, and 36 uses only 3 full feet without the extra half.
  8. The correct answer is B (The total price of the shirt is 27 dollars.): Find the tax by writing 8 percent as 0.08 and multiplying by 25, so 25 times 0.08 equals 2 dollars. Add the tax of 2 dollars to the original price of 25 to get 27 dollars. The total price is 27 dollars. The value 26 comes from using a 4 percent tax, and 28 or 29 come from a higher tax rate.
  9. The correct answer is B (The range of the cookie counts is 7.): The range is the largest value minus the smallest value, so 9 minus 2. That gives 7. The value 25 comes from adding all the cookie counts. The value 5 comes from counting the number of people instead.
  10. The correct answer is A (The surface area of the bin is 54 square metres): A cube has 6 square faces, and the area of each face is side times side.   11. The correct answer is B (The probability of drawing a yellow candy is 3 out of 10.): Count the total number of candies, so 7 green plus 3 yellow equals 10 candies. The favourable outcomes are the 3 yellow candies. So the probability is 3 out of 10. The other choices swap the numbers, or use the wrong total.
  12. The correct answer is B (The value of the entire expression is 14): Work the multiplication before the addition, so 2 times 3 equals 6. Then add 8 and 6 to get 14.   13. The correct answer is B (The enlarged width is 12 inches, since 4 times 3 is 12): A scale factor of 3 means each original length is multiplied by 3. Multiply the original width of 4 by the scale factor of 3. Four times 3 equals 12 inches, and 7 comes from adding instead of multiplying.
  14. The correct answer is C (The total area of both sections is 20 square metres.): Find the area of each rectangle and add them together.   15. The correct answer is C (The average speed of the cyclist is 20 kilometres per hour.): Average speed is the total distance divided by the total time.   1. The correct answer is A (The temperature at midnight is negative 1 degree Celsius.): Start at 5 degrees and subtract 9, so 5 minus 9 equals negative 4 degrees. The value negative 7 comes from subtracting 9 and 3 without adding the rise back, and 1 comes from an adding error.

  8. The correct answer is B (The probability of blue on the first spin is 1/3.): There are three equal sections and one is blue.   20. The correct answer is D (When the input is 5, the output is 17.): Look at how the output changes as the input grows by 1, and each output rises by 3. This means the output is 2 more than 3 times the input, so the rule is 3 times x plus 2. For an input of 5, compute 3 times 5 plus 2 to get 17. The output is 17, which is 3 more than the previous row's output of 14.
  21. The correct answer is 3/8 m: Change the quarter into eighths so the denominators match. One quarter equals two eighths, or 2 over 8. Subtract 2 eighths from 5 eighths to get 3 eighths. The fraction 3 over 8 is already in lowest terms so it is the final answer.
  22. The correct answer is C (The probability that the ball drawn is black is 1/3.): Probability is the number of favourable outcomes divided by the total number of outcomes. The choice of 1/2 comes from comparing black balls to the non-black balls instead of the total.
  23. The correct answer is 125 cm³: The volume of a cube is the side length times itself three times, so multiply 5 by 5 by 5. First 5 times 5 is 25, and then 25 times 5 is 125. The volume of the cube is 125 cubic centimetres. This equals the side length cubed, or 5 to the power of 3.
  24. The correct answer is A (The y value at x equals 5 is 10): Each time x increases by 1, y increases by 2, so the y value is twice the x value. At x equals 4 the point is at y 8, confirming that y goes up by 2 each step. Continuing one more step to x equals 5 gives y equals 10.
  25. The correct answer is A (The mean of the four quiz scores is 85.): Add all four scores first, so 82 plus 90 plus 74 plus 94 equals 340. A total of 340 divided by 4 is a clean 85 with no remainder.
  26. The correct answer is C (The probability of rolling a number greater than 4 is 1/3.): The numbers greater than 4 are 5 and 6, so there are two favourable outcomes.   28. The correct answer is C (Multiply the 4 and 3 first, then subtract from 20, giving 8): Multiply first, so 4 times 3 equals 12. Then subtract that product from 20, so 20 minus 12 equals 8. Doing the subtraction first is the common error that leads to 48.
  29. The correct answer is A (The middle value of the ordered list is the median.): The median is the middle value of a set of data when the values are written in order from lowest to highest. The mean is the average, which comes from adding and dividing. The mode is the value that appears most often, and the range is the difference between the highest and lowest. So the middle value is the median.
  30. The correct answer is D (The diameter of the circle is 18 centimetres): The diameter is twice the radius. The radius is 9 centimetres, so multiply by 2. Twice 9 is 18 centimetres. The value 4.5 is half the radius, and 9 is the radius itself.

Practice Test 15 Answer Key

  1. The correct answer is 11 degrees: The range is the largest value minus the smallest value. The largest temperature is 20 and the smallest is 9. Twenty minus 9 equals 11, so the range is 11 degrees.

The value 12 is the perimeter of one face, found by adding the four sides (3 plus 3 plus 3 plus 3), and the value 6 comes from adding just two of the sides.
  3. The correct answer is 18: In the order of operations, multiplication comes before addition and subtraction, so compute 3 times 4 first. That gives 12, then add 8 to get 20, and subtract 2 to get 18. Working left to right without the rule would give a different number. The value of the expression is 18.
  4. The correct answer is D (The probability of the result is 2 over 3): The even numbers are 2, 4, and 6, and the numbers greater than 4 are 5 and 6. Combine the two groups without double counting the 6, giving 2, 4, 5, and 6. That is 4 outcomes out of 6, which reduces to 2 over 3. The value 5 over 6 comes from counting the 6 twice.
  5. The correct answer is C (The rope is 350 centimetres long.): One metre equals 100 centimetres, so multiply the length in metres by 100. Multiply 3.5 by 100 to get 350.   6. The correct answer is C (The mode of the pet counts is 1.): Count how many times each value appears, and the number 1 appears three times.   7. The correct answer is 3/5: There are 6 red balls out of a total of 6 plus 4, which is 10 balls. The probability is 6 divided by 10. Six tenths simplifies to 3 fifths, so the probability of red is 3 fifths.

  13. The correct answer is B (The surface area of the box is 94 square centimetres.): The surface area is twice the sum of the three different face areas.   39. The correct answer is B (The amount of the sales tax is 5.20 dollars.): Write 13 percent as the decimal 0.13 and multiply it by the price, so 40 times 0.13. Since 0.13 times 40 is 5.20, the sales tax is 5.20 dollars. The value 4.00 comes from using 10 percent instead. The value 6.50 comes from using 16.25 percent, and 3.20 comes from using 8 percent.
  40. The correct answer is B (The area of the garden is 30 square metres.): Multiply the base and the height first, so 12 times 5 is 60. Then take half of that product, so 60 divided by 2 is 30. The area is 30 square metres. The value 60 in choice A comes from leaving out the half step, and 17 comes from adding the base and height.
  41. The correct answer is D (The discount amount is 6 dollars.): Write 15 percent as 0.15 and multiply by the price, so 0.15 times 40 is 6. The discount is 6 dollars. Practice Test 19 Answer Key

  1. The correct answer is B (The sum of the first four numbers in the pattern is 30.): Add the first four terms of the pattern together. Start with 2 plus 4 to get 6, then add 8 to get 14, then add 16 to get 30. The sum is 30. The pattern doubles each term, so the fifth term would be 32, which is larger than the sum of the first four.
  2. The correct answer is D (The total area of the room is 30 square metres.): Split the L shape into two rectangles and find each area. The first rectangle is 6 by 4, so its area is 24, and the second is 3 by 2, so its area is 6. Add the two areas, so 24 plus 6 is 30 square metres. The value 24 comes from using only one of the rectangles.
  3. The correct answer is B (The end point on the number line is 3.): Moving to the right on a number line means adding a positive number to the starting value. Start at negative 4 and add 7, so compute negative 4 plus 7. That gives 3, so the end point is 3. The choice of negative 11 comes from moving left instead of right, and 7 is just the size of the move.
  4. The correct answer is A (The interest that the account earns is 20 dollars.): Simple interest for one year is the rate times the principal. Four percent is 0.04, so multiply 0.04 by 500 dollars to get 20. The correct interest is 20 dollars. The choice of 2000 comes from multiplying the whole percent by the amount, and the choice of 200 comes from using 40 percent by mistake. The choice of 4 comes from using the rate as a dollar amount.
  5. The correct answer is D (The mean quiz score for the whole class is 10.): Add the five scores together, so 8 plus 9 plus 9 plus 10 plus 14 equals 50. There are five students, so divide 50 by 5 to get 10. The mean quiz score is 10. The value 9 is the mode and not the average, and 11 or 12 come from the wrong total or divisor.
  6. The correct answer is 40 metres: The perimeter of a rectangle is twice the length plus twice the width. Double the length of 15 to get 30, and double the width of 5 to get 10, then add them to get 40. The correct perimeter is 40 metres. Adding the four sides also gives 15 plus 15 plus 5 plus 5, which is 40.
  7. The correct answer is B (The perimeter equals two lengths plus two widths): The perimeter is the total distance around the rectangle, so both lengths and both widths are counted. Twice the length plus twice the width gives that total. Multiplying length by width gives area, not perimeter. The value from length times height times width is volume for a prism.
  8. The correct answer is 2 litres: Add the two solution volumes together, so 0.4 plus 1.6. The sum is 2.0, which is the same as 2 litres. The decimal places line up when the numbers are written vertically. A check shows 1.6 plus 0.4 goes to 2.0 exactly with no remainder.
  9. The correct answer is D (The 6 boxes weigh 21 kilograms in total.): Multiply the weight of one box by the number of boxes. Multiply 3.5 kilograms by 6, so 3 times 6 is 18 and 0.5 times 6 is 3, giving 21. The correct total weight is 21 kilograms. The choice of 18 comes from ignoring the 0.5 kilograms, and the choice of 21.5 comes from treating the 0.5 as a whole number. The choice of 24 comes from multiplying 4 by 6 by mistake.
  10. The correct answer is C (The mean of all five test scores is 78.): Add all five scores together, 70 plus 72 plus 74 plus 76 plus 98 equals 390.   11. The correct answer is D (The probability of drawing two red marbles is 3 out of 10.): The first draw has a 3 out of 5 chance of being red. After one red is removed, 2 red remain out of 4 marbles, so the second draw is 2 out of 4. Multiply 3 over 5 by 2 over 4 to get 6 over 20, which reduces to 3 over 10. The value 9 out of 25 comes from putting the first marble back.
  12. The correct answer is C (The area of the tabletop is 154 square cm.): The area of a circle is pi times the radius squared. The radius is 7 cm, so the radius squared is 49.   13. The correct answer is B (The mode of the choices is vanilla.): The mode is the category that appears most often in the data. Vanilla was chosen 8 times, chocolate 6 times, and strawberry 4 times, so vanilla has the highest count. The mode is vanilla. Chocolate and strawberry are each chosen fewer times than vanilla.
  14. The correct answer is A (The value of the expression 8 divided by 2 plus 6 times 2 is 16.): Do the division and multiplication before the addition. First 8 divided by 2 equals 4, and 6 times 2 equals 12. Then add 4 plus 12 to get 16. Choice A works from left to right without respecting the order of operations.
  15. The correct answer is B (The largest of the four decimals is 0.62.): Compare the tenths digits first, and 0.62 has a tenths digit of 6, matching 0.6, but 0.62 also has a hundredths digit of 2. The decimals 0.45 and 0.5 have tenths digits of 4 and 5, which are smaller. So 0.62 is the largest, since it has a 6 in the tenths place and another 2 following it.
  16. The correct answer is B (The poster is 12 inches wide.): A scale factor of 3 means every measurement is multiplied by 3. Multiply the original width of 4 inches by 3, so 4 times 3 is 12. The poster is 12 inches wide. The value 16 comes from squaring the original width (4 times 4), and 9 comes from adding the scale factor to the original height (6 plus 3).
  17. The correct answer is D (The volume of the cube is 64 cubic metres.): The volume of a cube is the edge length cubed, so multiply 4 by itself three times. Four times 4 is 16, and 16 times 4 is 64. The volume is 64 cubic metres. The value 16 comes from multiplying the edge by itself only twice.

Add 4 to the value of R, so 10 plus 4 is 14. The next point belongs at 14. The value 12 comes from adding only 2 each time.
  4. The correct answer is B (The value of x in the equation is 5.): Subtract 4 from both sides to get 3x equals 15. Divide both sides by 3 to isolate x, so x equals 15 over 3. Dividing 15 by 3 gives 5. Choosing 7 comes from adding 4 to 19 before dividing, which is the wrong order.
  5. The correct answer is 85 percent: Divide the correct count by the total, so 17 divided by 20 is 0.85. Then multiply by 100 to change the decimal into a percent, so 0.85 times 100 is 85. A quick check shows that 17 out of 20 is the same as 85 out of 100. The student got 85 percent correct.
  6. The correct answer is A (The total cost of the 6 packages of paper is 28.50 dollars.): Multiply the cost of one package by the number of packages, so 4.75 times 6. First multiply 4 times 6 to get 24, then 0.75 times 6 to get 4.50, and add those to get 28.50. The total is 28.50 dollars. Choice D uses 5 dollars per package, which overstates the price.
  7. The correct answer is D (The probability of picking a blue marble is 2/5.): There are 6 blue marbles out of 15 marbles in total, so the probability is 6 out of 15. Divide both parts of 6/15 by 3 to reduce it to 2/5. The value 3/5 comes from counting the red and green marbles together, which is 9 out of 15. The value 1/3 comes from forgetting one blue marble, and 1/2 treats the colours as equally likely.
  8. The correct answer is C (The discount that will be subtracted is 18.00 dollars.): Convert the percent discount to a decimal, so 40 percent becomes 0.40. Multiply the original price by that decimal, so 45 times 0.40 equals 18. The discount is 18.00 dollars, and the sale price would be the original minus the discount. The wrong choices come from using the wrong rate, such as 20 or 25 percent, instead of 40 percent.
  9. The correct answer is A (The surface area of the cube is 54 square centimetres.): Each square face has an area of 3 times 3, which is 9 square centimetres. There are six faces, so multiply 9 by 6 to get 54. The surface area is 54 square centimetres. The value 27 is the volume of the cube, and 36 comes from using four faces. The value 108 comes from doubling the correct total.
  10. The correct answer is C (The most common group of students has 0 pets at home.): Compare the counts of students for each pet number. The group with 0 pets has 8 students, which is more than 6, 4, or 2. So the most common group has 0 pets. Choice B has the next largest group with 6 students.
  11. The correct answer is 3: The mode is the value that appears most often in a data set. The number 3 shows up twice, while 1, 7, and 9 each appear once. So 3 is the mode. Frequency decides the mode, not the order or the size of the values.
  12. The correct answer is A (The car uses 6 litres for every 100 km driven.): Set up a ratio comparing litres to the distance driven, so 12 litres over 200 km. Divide the litres by the distance, then multiply by 100 to scale the distance to 100 km. So 12 times 100 divided by 200 gives 6, meaning the car uses 6 litres per 100 km. The other choices come from dividing in the wrong order or from an error scaling the numbers.
  13. The correct answer is B (The mean of the monthly sales is 21): The mean is the total divided by the number of months. Add 18, 24, 20, and 22 to get 84 jackets. Divide 84 by the 4 months to get 21. The value 24 is one of the monthly totals, not the average.
  14. The correct answer is B (The volume of the cylinder is 282.6 cubic centimetres.): Volume of a cylinder is pi times the radius squared times the height. Square the radius, so 3 times 3 is 9, then multiply by 10 to get 90. Multiply 3.14 by 90 to get 282.6 cubic centimetres. The value 94.2 comes from using the radius times the height without squaring the radius (3.14 times 3 times 10).
  15. The correct answer is D (The mode of the six plant heights is 25): The mode is the value that appears most often in a data set. The number 25 appears three times, while 30, 35, and 40 each appear once. Because 25 appears more than any other height, it is the mode. The other values are simply heights that show up only once.
  16. The correct answer is C (The area of the triangle is 35 square centimetres.): Multiply the base by the height, so 10 times 7 is 70. The area of a triangle is half of that product, so 70 divided by 2 is 35. The value 70 comes from forgetting to take half. Adding the base and height gives 17, which is not the area.
  17. The correct answer is A (The surface area of the box is 2000 square centimetres): There are 6 faces in total, and each pair of matching faces appears twice. The two large sides are 20 by 30 for 600 each, the two short sides are 20 by 8 for 160 each, and the two ends are 8 by 30 for 240 each. Doubling and adding those numbers gives 2000 square centimetres, while 1000 counts only one face from each pair.
  18. The correct answer is C (The range of the temperatures is 14 degrees): The range is the highest temperature minus the lowest temperature. The highest is 23 and the lowest is 9. Subtract 9 from 23 to get 14 degrees. The value 11 comes from subtracting a different pair of temperatures.
  19. The correct answer is B (The perimeter is 18 m, since 5 plus 6 plus 7 is 18): Perimeter is the total length of the sides added together. Add the three side lengths, so 5 plus 6 plus 7. Five plus 6 plus 7 equals 18 m, and 15 wrongly treats all sides as equal.
  20. The correct answer is A (Taking 25 percent off the price means the coat now costs $30): A 25 percent discount removes 25 percent of $40, and 25 percent of 40 is 10. Subtract the discount from the original price, so 40 minus 10 equals 30. The $10 choice is the amount saved, not the price paid, and $50 comes from adding the discount.
  21. The correct answer is 35 km: The scale tells you to multiply the map distance by 5. Multiply the map distance of 7 cm by the scale factor of 5. Seven times 5 equals 35, so the real distance is 35 kilometres.
  22. The correct answer is B (The mean number of books she read is 8.): Add all five monthly scores together, so 5 plus 7 is 12, plus 8 is 20, plus 9 is 29, plus 11 is 40.   23. The correct answer is A (The probability of green candy is 1 over 4): There are 3 green candies out of a total of 12 candies. The probability of green is 3 over 12. Divide the top and bottom by 3 to get 1 over 4. The value 1 over 3 would come from using the 4 yellow candies out of 12 instead.
  24. The correct answer is D (The perimeter of the flower bed is 26 metres.): Perimeter is the distance around the outside, so add the two lengths and the two widths.   26. The correct answer is A (The discount amount on the book is 3 dollars.): Change 15 percent to 0.15 and multiply by the price. Twenty times 0.15 equals 3 dollars off. The value 15 is the percent itself, not the amount in dollars. The value 2 comes from using a 10 percent discount by mistake.

  34. The correct answer is C (The value of the whole expression is 2): Work the multiplication first, so 4 times 2 equals 8. Then add negative 6 and 8, which means move from negative 6 up 8 units to land at 2. So the value is 2.   35. The correct answer is D (The median of the six numbers is 6.): Put the numbers in order from smallest to largest. The list has six values, so the middle falls between the third and fourth values, which are 5 and 7. Take the average of those two, 5 plus 7 divided by 2, to get 6. Choosing 5 or 7 alone ignores the fact that there are two middle values here.
  36. The correct answer is C (The lawn needs 126 packets of seed in total.): Find the area of the rectangle by multiplying the length by the width, so 14 times 9. This gives 126 square metres.   37. The correct answer is B (The doubled recipe needs 1 1/3 cups of sugar.): Doubling means multiplying by 2, so 2/3 times 2. Multiply the numerator by 2 to get 4/3. Practice Test 22 Answer Key

  1. The correct answer is A (Each person pays 7.20 dollars for the dinner.): Divide the total bill by the number of friends to find each share. Divide 36 dollars by 5 people, and 36 divided by 5 is 7.20. The correct amount per person is 7.20 dollars. The choice of 7 comes from rounding the answer down, and the choice of 30 comes from dividing 36 by 6 people to get 6, then multiplying that 6 by 5 to get 30. The choice of 6.20 comes from a division error.
  2. The correct answer is D (The surface area of the box is 94 square units.): Find the area of each different face: length times width is 20, length times height is 15, and width times height is 12. Each of these appears twice, so double each and add, giving 40 plus 30 plus 24. The surface area is 94 square units. Choosing 60 comes from finding the volume instead.
  3. The correct answer is A (The probability of drawing a red or green card is 1 out of 2.): There are 10 red and 5 green cards, so 15 cards are red or green. The box holds 10 plus 15 plus 5 equals 30 cards in total. So the probability is 15 out of 30, which simplifies to 1 out of 2. Choice C counts the blue cards too.
  4. The correct answer is D (The mode of the pet data set is 3.): The mode is the value that shows up the most. Count how many times each number appears, and 3 appears three times. The number 1 appears twice, while 2 and 8 appear once each, so 3 is the mode. This set also has a high value of 8, which is an outlier but does not change the mode.
  5. The correct answer is A (The length of the rectangle is 9 metres.): Substitute the known values into the perimeter formula, so 30 equals 2 times the length plus 2 times 6. Work out 2 times 6 to get 12, then subtract 12 from 30 to get 18. Divide 18 by 2 to find the length, which is 9 metres. The choice of 6 is the width, and 12 comes from not dividing by 2.
  6. The correct answer is B (The diameter of the pool is 10 metres in total.): The diameter is twice the radius, so multiply the radius by 2. The radius is 5 metres, so 5 times 2 equals 10. The diameter of the pool is 10 metres. Choosing 20 comes from doubling the radius twice by mistake.
  7. The correct answer is C (Each bag holds 6 apples.): Let each bag hold x apples, so 4 bags plus 3 extra apples equals 27. Subtract 3 from both sides to get 4x equals 24. Then divide both sides by 4, so x is 6. The value 8 comes from dividing 24 by 3 by mistake.
  8. The correct answer is 180 dollars: Multiply the monthly cost by the number of months the membership is kept. Take 30 dollars times 6, which equals 180. The total cost for 6 months is 180 dollars. Three times 6 is 18, and adding a zero gives 180.
  9. The correct answer is 10: The range is the largest value minus the smallest value. The largest number is 13 and the smallest number is 3. Subtract the smallest from the largest, so 13 minus 3 equals 10. The range of the set is 10.
  10. The correct answer is A (The median of the masses is 4 kilograms.): The list is already in order, so find the third of the five values, which is 4 kilograms. There are two masses below 4 and two masses above 4. The median is 4 kilograms. The values 3 and 5 are the second and fourth numbers, and 6 is the highest mass.
  11. The correct answer is D (The probability of picking a red marble is 3/5.): There are 6 red marbles, and there are 10 marbles altogether, so the probability is 6 out of 10. The value 1/2 comes from treating the two colours as equally likely, and 3/4 uses the wrong total.
  12. The correct answer is D (The area of the rectangular garden is 36 square metres.): The area of a rectangle is length times width. Multiply the length of 9 metres by the width of 4 metres. That gives 36, so the area is 36 square metres. The choice of 26 is the perimeter of the garden, which uses 2 times the length plus 2 times the width.
  13. The correct answer is A (The term at position 6 in the sequence is 25.): Substitute the position number into the rule, so for position 6 compute 4 times 6 plus 1. Multiplying 4 times 6 gives 24, then add 1 to get 25. The term at position 6 is 25. Choosing 21 comes from adding 4 to 17 by mistake.
  14. The correct answer is A (The sixth figure in the tile pattern uses 18 tiles in total.): The pattern adds 3 tiles each figure, so multiply the figure number by 3.   16. The correct answer is D (The value of the entire expression is 15): First work out the power and the multiplication. Two squared is 4, and 3 times 4 is 12. Then do 4 plus 12 minus 1, which equals 15. The value 17 comes from adding the 1 instead of subtracting it.
  17. The correct answer is B (The range of the daily email counts is 12.): The range is the largest value minus the smallest value in a data set. The largest daily count is 21 and the smallest is 9.   18. The correct answer is C (The score is equal to exactly 85 percent.): To change a decimal to a percent, multiply by 100, which moves the decimal point two places to the right.   23. The correct answer is C (The mean is changed more than the median by the outlier.): The mean uses every value, so adding the large 100 pulls the average up a lot. The median only considers the middle position, so one extreme value barely moves it. A single outlier therefore affects the mean far more than the median. Removing the outlier would bring the mean back down close to its original level.
  24. The correct answer is 28 m: The perimeter of a rectangle is twice the length plus twice the width. Double 8 to get 16 and double 6 to get 12. Add 16 and 12 to reach 28 metres. That is the total baseboard the room needs.

  36. The correct answer is D (The sales tax added to the meal is 3.90 dollars.): Sales tax is the rate times the price, so take 13 percent of 30 dollars. That side is the hypotenuse. The two shorter sides that form the right angle are the legs. The base and the height are names for the two legs, and the diagonal is a name used for four sided shapes.
