Practice Test 23 Answer Key

  1. The correct answer is 3/5: There are 5 red, 8 blue, and 7 green cards, so the deck has 20 cards in total. The cards that are not blue number 5 plus 7, which is 12. The probability is 12 out of 20, and dividing by 4 gives 3 over 5. The value would be 2 over 5 if the blue cards were counted instead.
  2. The correct answer is C (The diameter of the circle is 30 cm.): The diameter of a circle is twice the radius. The radius is 15 cm, so the diameter is 2 times 15. Two times 15 equals 30 cm, so the diameter is 30 cm. The other choices come from dividing the radius by 2, or from another error.
  3. The correct answer is -7 degrees: Start from the temperature at noon, which is positive 5 degrees. A drop of 12 means subtract 12, so subtract 12 from 5. Five minus 12 equals negative 7, so the evening temperature is negative 7 degrees.
  4. The correct answer is C (The car will use 28 litres of fuel for the 350 kilometre trip.): The car uses 8 litres for every 100 kilometres, so find how many hundreds of kilometres the trip has. Divide 350 by 100 to get 3.5, then multiply by 8 to get 28. The trip will use 28 litres of fuel. Choice A rounds the fuel use up instead of using the exact number.
  5. The correct answer is C (Area is measured in square units such as square metres.): Area measures the number of squares that fit inside a flat shape, so it is counted in square units. Square metres and square centimetres are examples of these units. Cubic units measure volume, which is the space inside a three dimensional object. Linear units such as metres measure length, and rate units measure one amount per another.
  6. The correct answer is 1.2 kg: Doubling a recipe means multiplying every amount by 2. Take the original 0.6 kilograms times 2, which gives 1.2 kilograms. The double batch needs 1.2 kilograms of sugar. One tenth of 0.6 is 0.06, so 0.6 plus 0.6 is 1.2.
  7. The correct answer is D (The percentage of students who walk is 30 percent.): Write the part over the whole as a fraction, so 12 out of 40. Divide 12 by 40 to get 0.30, then multiply by 100 to convert to a percent. The result is 30 percent, so 30 percent of the students walk to school. The wrong answers come from using the wrong numbers as the whole, or from an error dividing.
  8. The correct answer is C (The family spends 800 dollars on rent each month.): The Rent slice is 40 percent, so find 40 percent of the 2000 dollar budget. Forty percent is 0.40, and 0.40 times 2000 is 800. The correct amount spent on rent is 800 dollars. The choice of 500 is the Food amount at 25 percent, and the choice of 300 is the Savings amount at 15 percent. The choice of 400 is the Transport amount at 20 percent.
  9. The correct answer is 770 dollars: Multiply the hourly rate by the number of hours worked. Multiply 22 by 35, which is 22 times 30 plus 22 times 5, giving 660 plus 110. Adding those gives 770, so the worker earns 770 dollars for the week. This is the amount earned before deductions are taken out.
  10. The correct answer is C (The high 95 pulls the mean up above the other scores.): The mean is found by adding all the scores and dividing by the count. Because the 95 is far above the other four scores, it raises the total and pulls the mean upward. The mean with all five scores is 77.4, while the mean of just the four lower scores is 73. The median stays in the low 70s, so the outlier chiefly affects the mean.
  11. The correct answer is B (The fifth term of the square number pattern is 25 squares.): The pattern uses square numbers, where each term is the term number multiplied by itself. The first four terms are 1, 4, 9, and 16, so the fifth term is 5 times 5. That gives 25 squares. Choice C is the fourth term in the pattern.
  12. The correct answer is A (Each piece of ribbon is 2.5 metres long.): Divide the total length by the number of pieces, so 7.5 divided by 3. Because 3 times 2.5 equals 7.5, the quotient is 2.5 metres. Each piece is 2.5 metres long. The value 3.0 comes from dividing by 2.5 by mistake, and 2.25 comes from a shifting decimal error. The value 2.75 comes from a rounding error.
  13. The correct answer is B (The value of the given expression is 13.): Follow the order of operations and multiply first, so 4 times 2 is 8. Then add the 5 to get 13. The value 18 comes from adding 5 plus 4 first to get 9, then times 2. The value 9 comes from adding 5 and 4 and ignoring the multiplication.
  14. The correct answer is 14 dollars: The first hour is 5 dollars, and the remaining 3 hours cost 3 dollars each. Multiply 3 by 3 to get 9, then add the 5 dollar first hour to get 14. A quick check confirms that 5 plus 9 is 14. The total for 4 hours is 14 dollars.
  15. The correct answer is D (The total perimeter of the outline is 38 metres): The outline is the bottom, the two vertical sides, and the two sloping roof parts. Add the bottom of 12, the left side of 8, the left roof of 5, the right roof of 5, and the right side of 8. The sum is 38 metres. The value 45 double counts the shared base of the roof.
  16. The correct answer is D (The length of the missing leg is 12 centimetres.): In a right triangle, the square of the hypotenuse equals the sum of the squares of the two legs. Square the hypotenuse to get 225 and square the known leg to get 81. Subtract 81 from 225 to get 144, and the square root of 144 is 12. The value 6 comes from subtracting 9 from 15 directly, which ignores the formula.
  17. The correct answer is C (The probability of picking a red marble is 5/12.): There are 5 red marbles out of 12 marbles in total, so the probability is 5 out of 12. The probability is 5/12. The value 1/4 comes from using the yellow marbles, and 1/3 comes from using the green marbles. The value 1/2 comes from counting the red marbles as half the bag.
  18. The correct answer is 31: Find the difference between two consecutive terms, so 16 minus 11 equals 5. This means the rule adds 5 to each term. Add 5 to the last term, 26, to get 31. A common error is to add 4 instead, which would give 30.
  19. The correct answer is B (The real width of the room is 10 metres.): Use the scale by multiplying the drawing length by the scale factor. Each centimetre on the drawing equals 2 real metres, so multiply 5 centimetres by 2. Since 5 times 2 is 10, the real width of the room is 10 metres. The choice of 5 comes from ignoring the scale, and 7 comes from adding the scale value instead of multiplying.
  20. The correct answer is B (12 more books were lent out on Thursday): Read the height of the Thursday bar as 20 books and the Monday bar as 8 books. Subtract Monday from Thursday to find the difference. Twenty minus 8 equals 12 more books on Thursday. The value 20 is the Thursday total, and 8 is the Monday total.
  21. The correct answer is 81: The mean is the total of the scores divided by the number of tests. Add 72, 84, 78, and 90 to get 324. Divide 324 by the 4 tests to get 81. That is the average score for the term.
  22. The correct answer is 24 metres: The perimeter of a square is four times the side length, so multiply 6 metres by 4. That gives 24, so the perimeter is 24 metres. Adding the four sides of 6 metres also gives 6 plus 6 plus 6 plus 6, which is 24. The answer is 24 metres.
  23. The correct answer is A (The mean score of the five students is 8.): The mean is found by adding all the scores and dividing by the number of scores. Add 8, 9, 7, 10, and 6 to get 40, then divide by the 5 students. Dividing 40 by 5 gives 8, so the mean score is 8. The choice of 7 is the lowest score, and 9.5 comes from averaging only two of the values.
  24. The correct answer is C (A circle graph is the best choice for the data.): A circle graph, which is also called a pie chart, shows how each category compares with the whole as a part of a full circle. A line graph shows change over time, and a scatter plot shows the relationship between two variables. A histogram shows grouped data values along a number scale. So a circle graph is the best choice for parts of a whole.
  25. The correct answer is C (The next term in the pattern is 32.): The pattern in the sequence 4, 8, 16 doubles each term. After 16, the next term is 16 times 2, which equals 32. So the next number in the pattern is 32. The other choices come from adding a fixed amount instead of doubling.
  26. The correct answer is B (The ramp is 13 metres long along its sloping surface.): The ramp is the longest side of the right triangle, so square the two legs and add them. Five squared is 25, twelve squared is 144, and their sum is 169. The square root of 169 is 13, so the ramp is 13 metres long. Choice B multiplies the two legs together.
  27. The correct answer is B (The mean is pulled up more than the median is moved.): The mean uses every value in its addition, so one very large number pushes the mean upward a great deal. The median is the middle value when the list is in order, and the new high value simply becomes the largest data point without changing the middle. For these five values the median stays 48, while the mean jumps well above it. The other choices describe a flat median or a declining mean, which does not happen with one high value.
  28. The correct answer is D (The fare for the trip is 19 dollars.): Multiply the per kilometre rate by the distance, so 2 times 8 is 16. Then add the flat fee of 3, so 16 plus 3 is 19. The fare is 19 dollars. The value 16 comes from leaving out the flat fee, and 24 comes from multiplying 3 by 8 instead.
  29. The correct answer is B (The next term is 31, since 15 times 2 plus 1 is 31): Use the rule on the last term, which is 15. Multiply 15 by 2 to get 30, then add 1 to get 31. The 30 forgets the add 1 step, and the 17 adds 2 instead of doubling.
  30. The correct answer is A (The volume of the box is 36 cubic metres.): The volume of a rectangular box is length times width times height. Multiply 6 by 3 to get 18, then multiply 18 by 2 to get 36. The correct volume is 36 cubic metres. The choice of 18 comes from multiplying only the length and width, and the choice of 11 comes from adding the three dimensions. The choice of 30 comes from a multiplication error.
  31. The correct answer is 5 dollars: Write 5 percent as 0.05 and multiply it by the account balance. Take 0.05 times 100, which equals 5. The shopper earns 5 dollars of interest in one year. Five percent is 5 out of 100, so 5 percent of 100 dollars is simply 5 dollars.
  32. The correct answer is D (The grass area is 56 m2, since 60 minus 4 is 56): The whole lawn area is 10 times 6, which equals 60 square metres. The shed area is a square with area 2 times 2, which equals 4 square metres. Subtract the shed from the lawn, so 60 minus 4 equals 56 square metres.
  33. The correct answer is B (The median value of the set is 14.): Put the five numbers in order from smallest to largest, so 11, 12, 14, 15, 18. The median is the middle number when the list is ordered. The middle value is 14, so the median is 14. The other choices come from the mean, or from the wrong middle value.
  34. The correct answer is 9 pieces: Divide the total length of the ribbon by the length of each piece. Divide 4.5 by 0.5, which is the same as multiplying 4.5 by 2. Since 4.5 times 2 is 9, the roll makes 9 pieces. The answer is 9 pieces of ribbon.
  35. The correct answer is D (The real wall is 20 metres long in real life.): Use the scale that 1 centimetre equals 4 metres. Multiply the drawing length by the scale factor, so 5 times 4. This gives 20, so the real wall is 20 metres long. Choosing 9 comes from adding 5 and 4 instead of multiplying.
  36. The correct answer is A (The rope is 60 inches long in total.): Multiply the length in feet by the number of inches in one foot to change units. Multiply 5 feet by 12 inches per foot, which gives 60. The rope is 60 inches long. The choice of 17 comes from adding 5 and 12 instead of multiplying, which would give the wrong unit change.
  37. The correct answer is B (The discount shown on the jacket is $15): A discount of 25 percent of 60 means multiplying by 0.25. Multiply 60 by 0.25 to get 15 dollars off. That 15 is the discount amount. The value 45 is the price after the discount, not the discount itself.
  38. The correct answer is 8 centimetres: The Pythagorean theorem says the square of the hypotenuse equals the sum of the squares of the two legs. Square 6 to get 36 and square 10 to get 100, then subtract 36 from 100 to get 64. The square root of 64 is 8, so the other leg is 8 centimetres long.
  39. The correct answer is D (The discount on the jacket is 20 dollars off the price.): Multiply the original price by the percent written as a decimal, so 80 times 0.25. Since 80 times 0.25 equals 20, the discount is 20 dollars. Choice B treats 25 percent as 25 dollars, which is the wrong interpretation. Choice C is the sale price after the discount.
  40. The correct answer is B (The length of the hypotenuse is 13 centimetres.): The Pythagorean theorem says the square of the hypotenuse equals the sum of the squares of the two legs. Square 5 to get 25 and square 12 to get 144, then add them to get 169. The square root of 169 is 13, so the hypotenuse is 13 centimetres. The value 17 comes from adding the two legs, and 7 comes from subtracting them.
  41. The correct answer is D (The volume of the freezer is 2 cubic metres.): The volume of a rectangular box is length times width times height. Multiply 2 by 1 to get 2, then multiply 2 by 1 to get 2. The correct volume is 2 cubic metres. The choice of 1 comes from using only one of the dimensions, and the choice of 4 comes from doubling the volume. The choice of 3 comes from adding only two of the dimensions (2 + 1).

Practice Test 24 Answer Key

  1. The correct answer is 65: There are five scores, so the median is the middle one when they are arranged in order. The ordered list is 55, 60, 65, 70, and 100. The third value is 65, which is the median. The score of 100 is an outlier, but it does not change the middle position.
  2. The correct answer is A (The number of students who signed up is 6.): Change 15 percent to the decimal 0.15 and multiply by the total number of students. Multiply 40 by 0.15 to get 6, since 0.15 times 40 is 6. So 6 students signed up for the field trip. The choice of 4 comes from taking 10 percent of 40, and 8 comes from taking 20 percent.
  3. The correct answer is A (The circumference of the wheel is 31.4 centimetres.): The circumference is pi times the diameter, so multiply 3.14 by 10. The product 3.14 times 10 is 31.4 centimetres. The value 78.5 is the area of a circle with this diameter, not the circumference. The value 314 comes from a missing decimal shift, and 15.7 is the radius times pi instead of the diameter times pi.
  4. The correct answer is B (The total area of the L-shaped wall is 24 square metres.): Find the area of each rectangle and add them. The large rectangle is 6 times 3, which is 18, and the small rectangle is 2 times 3, which is 6. Adding 18 and 6 gives 24 square metres. The choice of 18 is only the large rectangle, and the choice of 30 comes from mistaking the small rectangle as 2 by 6. The choice of 36 comes from doubling the large rectangle by mistake (18 x 2 = 36).
  5. The correct answer is 13: Work out the power first, so 3 squared is 3 times 3, which is 9. Then add 4 to 9 to get 13. The value of 3 squared plus 4 is 13. Doing the addition first would give 49, which does not follow the order of operations.
  6. The correct answer is C (The total change is a negative 18 degrees): The temperature drops by 6 degrees in each of 3 hours, so multiply 6 times 3. Six times 3 is 18. Because the temperature is getting colder, the change is negative 18 degrees. The value 18 without the sign would mean a rise in temperature.
  7. The correct answer is C (The host needs 8 cups of sparkling water for 12 cups of juice.): The ratio of juice to sparkling water is 3 to 2, so the fractions 3/2 and 12/x are equal. Cross multiply to get 3 times x equals 24, then divide 24 by 3 to find x. That gives 8, so the host needs 8 cups of sparkling water. The choice of 24 comes from doubling 12, and 6 comes from dividing by 2.
  8. The correct answer is A (The new temperature after the drop is negative 14 degrees.): The temperature drops, so the value moves farther below zero. Start at negative 6 and subtract 8, which is the same as negative 6 plus negative 8. Adding them gives negative 14. The correct temperature is negative 14 degrees. The choice of negative 2 comes from subtracting in the wrong direction, and the choice of 2 comes from dropping the negative sign. The choice of 14 comes from using a positive value.
  9. The correct answer is C (You get a total of 6 full glasses.): To divide by a fraction, multiply by its reciprocal, so 3/4 divided by 1/8 becomes 3/4 times 8. Multiply the numerators, 3 times 8 equals 24, and multiply the denominators, 4 times 1 equals 4, giving 24/4. Dividing 24 by 4 gives 6. Choosing 5 comes from a division slip such as treating 3/4 as 5/8, since 5/8 divided by 1/8 is 5.
  10. The correct answer is 9 cm: The area of a triangle is one half of the base times the height. So the height is 2 times the area divided by the base. Two times 90 is 180, and 180 divided by 20 is 9. The height of the flag is 9 centimetres.
  11. The correct answer is A (Each term is twice the term before it.): Compare each term with the previous one, so 2 becomes 4, which is twice as much. Doubling 4 gives 8, and doubling 8 gives 16. The rule is multiply by 2 each time. Choosing the add 2 rule would produce 2, 4, 6, 8, which does not match the sequence.
  12. The correct answer is A (The value of 5 times 2 squared is 20.): Work out the power first, so 2 squared is 2 times 2, which is 4. Then multiply 4 by 5 to get 20. The value 20 is the result of 5 times 2 squared. The value 25 comes from multiplying 5 by 5, and 10 comes from multiplying 5 by 2. The value 22 comes from adding instead of multiplying.
  13. The correct answer is D (The fraction three quarters equals exactly 75 percent): A percent is a fraction out of 100. Multiply the fraction 3 over 4 by 25 over 25 so the denominator becomes 100, giving 75 over 100. That fraction is written as 75 percent. The value 25 percent would be the fraction 1 over 4 instead.
  14. The correct answer is A (The cost for 6 hours is 23 dollars): Substitute 6 for t, so the rule becomes 5 plus 3 times 6. Three times 6 equals 18, and 5 plus 18 equals 23. The 18 is only the 3 times 6 part, and 30 comes from multiplying the whole expression by 6.
  15. The correct answer is D (The percent who chose the museum is 40 percent.): Divide the museum votes by the total votes, so 8 divided by 20 equals 0.40. Multiply by 100 to turn the decimal into a percent, giving 40 percent. The value 50 percent would mean exactly half of the class voted for the museum. The value 35 percent is lower than the true share because it uses a different total.
  16. The correct answer is D (The border that is needed measures 34 metres): The perimeter is the distance all the way around a rectangle. Double the length to get 24, double the width to get 10, then add them to reach 34. The value 60 comes from multiplying the length by the width, which gives area instead. The value 17 comes from adding just one length and one width.
  17. The correct answer is C (The median rainfall reading is 5 millimetres.): Put the seven readings in order from smallest to largest, and the median is the value in the middle. The middle of the ordered list is the fourth value, which is 5. No averaging is needed for the median. The value 4 comes from picking the third value, and 21 comes from the extreme high reading.
  18. The correct answer is 30: Double the length to get 18 and double the width to get 12. Add these together, so 18 plus 12 equals 30. The perimeter of the rectangle is 30 units. A common error is to multiply length times width, which gives 54, the area.
  19. The correct answer is 42 cm2: The area of a triangle is one half of the base times the height. So the area is one half of 12 times 7. One half of 12 is 6, and 6 times 7 equals 42. The triangle has an area of 42 square centimetres.
  20. The correct answer is D (The height of the triangle is 6 centimetres in total.): The area formula is one half of the base times the height. Multiply 8 by one half to get 4, so 24 equals 4 times the height. Divide 24 by 4 to get 6, so the height is 6 centimetres. Choice C is double the correct height.
  21. The correct answer is A (The courier delivered 180 packages over the two days.): Tuesday has 3 times as many packages as Monday, so multiply 45 by 3 to get 135. Add Monday's 45 and Tuesday's 135 to get 180. The correct total is 180 packages. The choice of 135 is only the Tuesday total, and the choice of 90 comes from doubling Monday instead of tripling it. The choice of 200 comes from a miscount.
  22. The correct answer is A (The stack is 3.0 centimetres tall in total.): Multiply the thickness of one sheet by the number of sheets, so 0.12 times 25. Since 0.12 times 100 is 12, then 0.12 times 25 is one quarter of that, which is 3. The stack is 3.0 centimetres tall. Choosing 0.30 comes from placing the decimal point one place too far to the left.
  23. The correct answer is D (The height of the plant at week 8 is 16 centimetres.): The plant grows 4 centimetres every 2 weeks, which is 2 centimetres per week. From week 6 the height is 12, so add another 4 centimetres to reach week 8. That gives 16 centimetres, so the height at week 8 is 16 centimetres. The choice of 12 is the height at week 6, and 20 comes from adding 8 instead of 4.
  24. The correct answer is B (The baker needs 1/3 of a cup of sugar.): Half of a recipe means multiplying the original amount by 1/2. Multiply 1/2 times 2/3, which gives 2/6 and simplifies to 1/3. The baker needs 1/3 of a cup of sugar. The choice of 2/3 is the full amount without reducing the recipe, and 1/6 comes from multiplying the denominators incorrectly.
  25. The correct answer is D (The sale price of the phone is 360 dollars.): A 20 percent discount means the customer pays 80 percent of the price, so multiply 450 by 0.80 to get 360. Or take 20 percent of 450, which is 90, and subtract it from 450 to get 360. The correct sale price is 360 dollars. The choice of 90 is the discount amount, and the choice of 430 comes from subtracting 20 dollars instead of 20 percent. The choice of 405 comes from using a 10 percent discount.
  26. The correct answer is C (The value of n is 33, since 45 minus 12 is 33): Undo the addition of 12 by subtracting 12 from both sides. So n equals 45 minus 12. Forty five minus 12 is 33, and the 57 comes from adding instead of subtracting.
  27. The correct answer is C (The median number of boxes shipped per day is 12.): The median is the middle value when the numbers are placed in order from least to greatest. The ordered list is 6, 8, 12, 15, and 16, so the third value is the median. The third value is 12, so the median is 12 boxes per day. The choice of 11.4 is the mean, and 57 is the total of the five values.
  28. The correct answer is B (Sofia receives 50 cents in change.): Write five dollars as 5.00 and subtract the price 4.50, so 5.00 minus 4.50 is 0.50. That leaves 50 cents in change. The value 25 cents comes from halving the change by mistake, and 1 dollar and 50 cents comes from subtracting 3.50 instead. The value 55 cents comes from a subtraction error.
  29. The correct answer is C (The amount of the discount is 15 dollars.): Write 25 percent as the decimal 0.25 and multiply it by the original price of 60. The product 0.25 times 60 is 15, so the discount amount is 15 dollars. The sale price after the discount would be 45 dollars, which is a separate amount. The value 25 is the percent itself, not the money amount, and 10 comes from a different percent.
  30. The correct answer is B (The chef can fill 6 bowls with the soup.): Divide the total amount of soup by the amount in each bowl, so 3/4 divided by 1/8. Dividing by a fraction means multiplying by its reciprocal, so 3/4 times 8 gives 24/4. Since 24/4 is 6, the chef can fill 6 bowls. The choice of 8 comes from using the denominator as the number of bowls.
  31. The correct answer is A (The volume of the fish tank is 24000 cubic centimetres.): The volume of a rectangular tank is length times width times height. Multiply 40 by 20 to get 800, then multiply 800 by 30 to get 24000. The correct volume is 24000 cubic centimetres. The choice of 2400 comes from missing a zero, and the choice of 800 comes from multiplying only the length and width. The choice of 90 comes from adding the three dimensions by mistake.
  32. The correct answer is B (More study time is tied to higher scores): The points climb from the lower left toward the upper right, which shows a positive trend. As study hours increase, the test scores also tend to increase.
  33. The correct answer is A (The range of the given set of marks is 7.): Identify the largest mark and the smallest mark in the data set. The largest is 15 and the smallest is 8. Subtract the smallest from the largest, 15 minus 8 equals 7. Choosing 12 comes from using an interior value instead of the two extremes.
  34. The correct answer is 4 students: The range is the largest value minus the smallest value. The largest count is 5 and the smallest is 1, so subtract 1 from 5 to get 4. A quick check confirms the spread between the highest and lowest day is 4. The range is 4 students.
  35. The correct answer is A (The height of the wall is 2.5 metres.): Convert the height from centimetres to metres by dividing by 100. The wall is 250 cm tall, and there are 100 cm in a metre. Dividing 250 by 100 gives 2.5, so the wall is 2.5 metres high. The other choices come from dividing by a different factor, or from a conversion error.
  36. The correct answer is B (The amount of water needed is 12 cups): The recipe keeps a ratio of 5 cups of flour to 3 cups of water. Divide 20 by 5 to find there are 4 groups. Multiply 3 cups of water by those 4 groups to get 12. The value 15 would come from a different scaling error, not from reversing the ratio.
  37. The correct answer is 60 km: Distance equals speed multiplied by time. Multiply 15 kilometres per hour by 4 hours to get 60 kilometres. The hours cancel out when the units are multiplied. So the cyclist rides 60 kilometres in total.
  38. The correct answer is B (The tip on a 50 dollar meal at 18 percent is 9 dollars.): Write 18 percent as 0.18 and multiply by the meal cost of 50 dollars. Take 0.18 times 50 to get 9, so the tip is 9 dollars. A quick check uses 10 percent of 50 is 5 and 8 percent of 50 is 4, and 5 plus 4 equals 9. Choice A is 20 percent of the meal.
  39. The correct answer is 28 square metres: The area of a rectangle is length times width, so multiply 7 metres by 4 metres. Seven times 4 is 28, so the area is 28 square metres. The patio covers 28 square metres, so that is the amount of stone needed.
  40. The correct answer is C (The scatterplot shows a negative relationship between the two variables.): When one value goes up and the other goes down, the points fall from left to right, which is a negative relationship. Here hotter days come with fewer hot drinks sold. Choice B would mean that both values rise together. A negative relationship means the two variables move in opposite directions.
  41. The correct answer is C (The area of the triangular banner is 40 square centimetres.): The area of a triangle is one half of the base times the height. Multiply the base of 10 by the height of 8 to get 80, then divide by 2. Half of 80 is 40, so the area is 40 square centimetres. The choice of 80 comes from multiplying base times height without taking half, and 18 comes from adding the base and the height.
